deception, supporting the warning side of f02."},{"label":"strong","ref":"33:22","note":"What was promised is recognized when its event appears, directly supporting f02."},{"label":"medium","ref":"34:9","note":"Earth-swallowing and falling sky fragments form a direct catastrophe parallel to f01."},{"label":"weak","ref":"35:1","note":"Creator-of-heavens language supplies an indirect f01 origin frame."},{"label":"medium","ref":"35:5","note":"The true promise is set against deception, a secondary f02 reliability parallel."},{"label":"strong","ref":"35:41","note":"The heavens are held from collapse, directly clarifying the stability lost in f01."},{"label":"strong","ref":"36:52","note":"A realized event is identified as what was promised, directly supporting f02."},{"label":"medium","ref":"40:64","note":"The sky as a constructed roof supplies a structural antecedent for f01."},{"label":"medium","ref":"40:77","note":"God's promise is true and some warned outcomes are shown, supporting f02."},{"label":"medium","ref":"41:12","note":"The ordered seven heavens provide a distant normal-order contrast to f01."},{"label":"strong","ref":"45:32","note":"The true promise and the Hour are affirmed together, directly supporting f02."},{"label":"strong","ref":"48:27","note":"A declared vision is fulfilled in truth, a close word-to-event f02 parallel."},{"label":"strong","ref":"50:6","note":"The built sky's absence of fissures is the direct precondition reversed by f01."},{"label":"medium","ref":"51:47","note":"The sky's construction supplies a secondary structural frame for f01."},{"label":"medium","ref":"52:44","note":"Falling fragments from the sky extend f01's catastrophe imagery."},{"label":"strong","ref":"55:37","note":"Direct f01 parallel: the sky splits and changes state."},{"label":"medium","ref":"67:3","note":"The flawless layered heavens establish the stable order undone in f01."},{"label":"strong","ref":"69:16","note":"Direct f01 parallel: the sky splits and becomes frail."},{"label":"medium","ref":"70:8","note":"The sky's transformation into a molten condition extends the cosmic-collapse reading."},{"label":"medium","ref":"70:44","note":"The appointed day is identified as what they were promised, supporting f02."},{"label":"weak","ref":"71:15","note":"Layered heavens provide only a general structural antecedent for f01."},{"label":"strong","ref":"77:7","note":"What is promised is stated as inevitable, directly reinforcing f02."},{"label":"weak","ref":"78:12","note":"Strongly built heavens provide a distant normal-order contrast to f01."},{"label":"medium","ref":"78:17","note":"The Day of Decision as an appointment supports f02's fixed-time dimension."},{"label":"weak","ref":"79:27","note":"The sky's construction supplies only a broad antecedent for f01."},{"label":"weak","ref":"88:18","note":"The raised sky gives a general structural background for f01."}],"channel_subchannels_anchored_here":[{"parent_channel":"P2 - Deferred Appointment and Irreversible Arrival","parent_semantic_invariant":"Delay belongs to a fixed schedule whose promised event ultimately arrives, occurs, and acquires the force of an accomplished act.","parent_surprising_reach":"Temporal reckoning extends into moon stations, lunar halos, star markers, weekday naming, and the contrast between first, later, and terminal positions.","key":"A","name":"A short reprieve before an appointed time","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A period of calm delay is granted, but it remains bounded by a specified appointment.","active_motifs":"deliberate delay and absence of haste (`quranic:root_001452:B001/m01`); a small interval (`quranic:root_001251:B001/m01`); postponement to a later time (`quranic:root_000019:B002/m01`); an appointment fixed by time or place (`quranic:root_001662:B003/m01`); a duration of time (`quranic:root_001700:B002/m01`); an event present in time (`quranic:root_001332:B001/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `مَهِّلْهُمْ/قَلِيلًا`; 73:14,17 `يَوْمَ/يَوْمًا`; 73:18 `كَانَ/وَعْدُهُ`; 73:20 `ءَاخَرُونَ/يَكُونُ`","synthesis":"Delay is not cancellation but a measured opening before an appointment. “Later” and temporal occurrence keep the reprieve inside a schedule whose endpoint remains intact.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:14","73:18","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Deferred Appointment and Irreversible Arrival","parent_semantic_invariant":"Delay belongs to a fixed schedule whose promised event ultimately arrives, occurs, and acquires the force of an accomplished act.","parent_surprising_reach":"Temporal reckoning extends into moon stations, lunar halos, star markers, weekday naming, and the contrast between first, later, and terminal positions.","key":"B","name":"Promise becoming an accomplished event","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A verbal commitment advances from expectation through arrival to enacted fact.","active_motifs":"a promise opening expectation (`quranic:root_001662:B001/m01`); threatening promise (`quranic:root_001662:B002/m01`); coming and arrival (`quranic:root_000009:B001/m01`); calamity arriving upon a person (`quranic:root_000009:B011/m01`); return to an outcome or final meaning (`quranic:root_000067:B002/m01`); occurrence in time (`quranic:root_001332:B001/m01`); an enacted deed (`quranic:root_001167:B001/m01`); a calamitous day or event (`quranic:root_001700:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `أُو۟لِى`; 73:14,17 `يَوْمَ/يَوْمًا`; 73:18 `كَانَ/وَعْدُهُ/مَفْعُولًا`; 73:20 `ءَاتُ/يَكُونُ`","synthesis":"The sequence closes the distance between saying and reality. Arrival supplies the transition, occurrence supplies presence, action realizes it, and outcome names what the event finally becomes or means.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:14","73:18","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Deferred Appointment and Irreversible Arrival","parent_semantic_invariant":"Delay belongs to a fixed schedule whose promised event ultimately arrives, occurs, and acquires the force of an accomplished act.","parent_surprising_reach":"Temporal reckoning extends into moon stations, lunar halos, star markers, weekday naming, and the contrast between first, later, and terminal positions.","key":"C","name":"Reading the celestial calendar","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Moon position, halo, stellar mansion, and named days provide observable markers for scheduled time.","active_motifs":"the moon taking successive stations (`quranic:root_000018:B008/m01`); a lunar halo (`quranic:root_001613:B003/m01`); a lunar mansion of three stars (`quranic:root_001096:B006/m01`); a guiding mark or landmark (`quranic:root_001040:B002/m01`); the named day Tuesday (`quranic:root_000203:B006/m01`); daylight as a bounded unit (`quranic:root_001700:B001/m01`); an observable sign promising a future condition (`quranic:root_001662:B006/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:14,17 `يَوْمَ/يَوْمًا`; 73:16,19 `أَخَذْ/ٱتَّخَذَ`; 73:18 `وَعْدُهُ`; 73:20 `ثُلُثَىِ/عَلِمَ/غَفُورٌ`","synthesis":"These motifs form a practical timekeeping scene: celestial position is observed, marked, grouped, and translated into named units. The promised day is thus pressured by a latent calendar whose signs make an appointment legible.","ayah_refs":["73:14","73:16","73:18","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Failure of Earthly, Celestial, and Hydraulic Containment","parent_semantic_invariant":"Structures that normally support, cover, or channel material lose integrity and release what they hold.","parent_surprising_reach":"Mountain collapse, sky rupture, flood, runoff, and hidden erosion are variants of one containment failure across solid, aerial, and liquid media.","key":"B","name":"The overhead cover splitting open","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"What normally rises above and covers the world tears and exposes the space beneath.","active_motifs":"sky or overhead canopy (`quranic:root_000745:B004/m01`); opening and splitting (`quranic:root_001165:B001/m01`); the lower realm opposite the sky (`quranic:root_000025:B001/m01`); a covering darkness or sea (`quranic:root_001307:B002/m01`); basic covering and concealment (`quranic:root_001307:B001/m01`); an exposed body with no cover from the sky (`quranic:root_000994:B004/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:13 `عَذَابًا`; 73:14 `ٱلْأَرْضُ`; 73:17 `كَفَرْتُمْ`; 73:18 `ٱلسَّمَآءُ/مُنفَطِرٌ`","synthesis":"Sky and earth establish the vertical enclosure; splitting removes its upper boundary. The covering senses of `ك ف ر` sharpen the reversal: the same lexical field that hides now supplies the cover that can no longer remain closed.","ayah_refs":["73:13","73:14","73:17","73:18"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Message Tested by Testimony and Enactment","parent_semantic_invariant":"A communicated claim is tested by witness, enacted fact, and the quality of its recipients’ response.","parent_surprising_reach":"Falsehood appears not only as lying but as a failed charge, deceptive cloth, rumor-induced panic, and fabricated composition.","key":"B","name":"Denial, rumor, and deceptive representation","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Recipients reject the message while false representations circulate and generate disturbance.","active_motifs":"falsehood opposed to truth (`quranic:root_001290:B001/m01`); attributing falsehood to a speaker (`quranic:root_001290:B002/m01`); a deceptive dyed or patterned cloth (`quranic:root_001290:B009/m01`); denial and concealment of truth (`quranic:root_001307:B003/m01`); alarming or false rumor (`quranic:root_000545:B003/m01`); fabricated discourse made without a prior model (`quranic:root_001167:B004/m01`); disobedience to authority (`quranic:root_001022:B001/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `ٱلْمُكَذِّبِينَ`; 73:14 `تَرْجُفُ`; 73:16 `عَصَىٰ`; 73:17 `كَفَرْتُمْ`; 73:18 `مَفْعُولًا`","synthesis":"The scene distinguishes several failures of representation: direct lying, accusing another of lying, visual deception, fabricated composition, and rumor. Disobedience is the behavioral outcome when that representational refusal is directed at an envoy.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:14","73:16","73:17","73:18"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Message Tested by Testimony and Enactment","parent_semantic_invariant":"A communicated claim is tested by witness, enacted fact, and the quality of its recipients’ response.","parent_surprising_reach":"Falsehood appears not only as lying but as a failed charge, deceptive cloth, rumor-induced panic, and fabricated composition.","key":"C","name":"Commitment verified by action","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A promise is judged true when a corresponding act occurs and becomes observable fact.","active_motifs":"verbal commitment (`quranic:root_001662:B001/m01`); mutual commitment (`quranic:root_001662:B004/m01`); an enacted deed (`quranic:root_001167:B001/m01`); occurrence in time (`quranic:root_001332:B001/m01`); knowledgeable disclosure (`quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`); finding an object as present (`quranic:root_001626:B001/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:14,18,20 `كَانَتِ/كَانَ/يَكُونُ`; 73:18 `وَعْدُهُ/مَفْعُولًا`; 73:20 `يَعْلَمُ/عَلِمَ/تَجِدُوهُ`","synthesis":"Promise and action are not merged: promise establishes the claim, occurrence gives it temporal presence, knowledge recognizes it, and finding supplies experiential verification. The “fulfilled promise” is therefore a completed evidentiary chain.","ayah_refs":["73:14","73:18","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Covering, Protection, and Exposure","parent_semantic_invariant":"A covering may preserve what is within, conceal what should be known, or fail and expose its contents.","parent_surprising_reach":"Protective barrier, fruit casing, eye film, garment, roof edge, forgiveness, disbelief, and sky cover all turn on the ethics and mechanics of concealment.","key":"C","name":"Roof, drape, and sky torn open","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Overhead and hanging coverings are lowered or removed until the protected interior stands exposed.","active_motifs":"sky as overhead canopy (`quranic:root_000745:B004/m01`); splitting open (`quranic:root_001165:B001/m01`); roof terrace without a parapet (`quranic:root_000015:B003/m01`); exposure directly beneath the sky (`quranic:root_000994:B004/m01`); lowering a hanging cover (`quranic:root_000672:B004/m01`); draping a garment (`quranic:root_001163:B005/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:13 `عَذَابًا`; 73:18 `ٱلسَّمَآءُ/مُنفَطِرٌ`; 73:19-20 `سَبِيلًا/سَبِيلِ/أَجْرًا/فَضْلِ`","synthesis":"Canopy, parapet, and drape define protected edges; split, exposure, and lowering undo them. The ruptured sky becomes the largest instance of a familiar architectural failure.","ayah_refs":["73:13","73:18","73:19"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Compressed Life Cycle","parent_semantic_invariant":"Birth, growth, maturation, and aging can be accelerated until distant life stages collapse into one event.","parent_surprising_reach":"The child-to-grey transformation is supported by a detailed reproductive ledger of womb, afterbirth, childbirth blood, near delivery, tooth eruption, and derived new life.","key":"C","name":"Emergence, growth, and bodily markers","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A newly created thing emerges, grows, and displays visible markers of development.","active_motifs":"generation of one thing from another (`quranic:root_001683:B005/m01`); creating and beginning (`quranic:root_001165:B002/m01`); tooth eruption (`quranic:root_001165:B007/m01`); growth and increase (`quranic:root_000637:B001/m01`); yield and production (`quranic:root_000009:B007/m01`); making or creating (`quranic:root_000248:B001/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:17 `يَجْعَلُ/ٱلْوِلْدَٰنَ`; 73:18 `مُنفَطِرٌ`; 73:20 `ءَاتُ/زَّكَوٰةَ`","synthesis":"Creation is followed by derived existence, organic increase, productive yield, and the visible eruption of a tooth. The normal sequence provides the baseline that the sudden greying violently overrides.","ayah_refs":["73:17","73:18","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Hearth and Meal Preparation","parent_semantic_invariant":"A meal is made by assembling a hearth, applying heat, cutting material, and controlling concentration.","parent_surprising_reach":"A pot, third hearthstone, lifting cloth, cut meat, fire, hastily baked dough, reduced drink, and measured sips form an ordinary kitchen whose operations are inverted by the punitive meal.","key":"A","name":"Building and working the hearth","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A pot is set on three supports, heated, handled with a cloth, and supplied with cut food.","active_motifs":"cooking pot and its contents (`quranic:root_001205:B007/m01`); the third hearthstone completing a support (`quranic:root_000203:B007/m01`); cloth used to lower or lift a hot pot (`quranic:root_000248:B007/m01`); cutting meat (`quranic:root_001638:B002/m01`); exposure to cooking fire (`quranic:root_000879:B001/m01`); blazing heat (`quranic:root_000225:B001/m01`); dough baked before proper maturation (`quranic:root_001165:B006/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `مَهِّلْهُمْ`; 73:12 `جَحِيمًا`; 73:14 `ٱلْجِبَالُ`; 73:17 `يَجْعَلُ`; 73:18 `مُنفَطِرٌ`; 73:20 `ثُلُثَهُ/يُقَدِّرُ/صَّلَوٰةَ`","synthesis":"Each motif occupies a kitchen role: supports stabilize the vessel, fire heats it, cloth protects the handler, cutting portions the ingredient, and premature baking names failed timing. The scene supplies the ordinary productive counterpart to infernal heat.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:12","73:14","73:17","73:18","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Prestige, Luxury, and Its Reversal","parent_semantic_invariant":"Social distinction is constructed through comfort, rank, reputation, display, and claims of superiority, then exposed to reversal.","parent_surprising_reach":"Crown, leaders-as-mountains, fame, padded bodily display, dispersal, abasement, and scarcity make luxury a fragile social construction rather than a stable possession.","key":"B","name":"Display, fame, and arrogant enlargement","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Status is amplified through bodily display, crown, public fame, elite leadership, and explicit superiority claims.","active_motifs":"arrogance and self-enlargement (`quranic:root_001029:B006/m01`); padding used to enlarge bodily appearance (`quranic:root_001029:B008/m01`); crown as a covering of kingship (`quranic:root_001307:B015/m01`); good fame spreading (`quranic:root_000745:B008/m01`); public reputation (`quranic:root_000516:B007/m01`); leaders likened to mountains (`quranic:root_000217:B012/m01`); claiming superiority over peers (`quranic:root_001163:B004/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:14 `ٱلْجِبَالُ`; 73:17 `كَفَرْتُمْ`; 73:18 `ٱلسَّمَآءُ`; 73:19 `تَذْكِرَةٌ`; 73:20 `فَضْلِ/أَعْظَمَ`","synthesis":"Status is literally made larger, covered by a crown, broadcast as fame, and stabilized by mountain-like leaders. The scene exposes prestige as crafted display and social repetition, not intrinsic magnitude.","ayah_refs":["73:14","73:17","73:18","73:19","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Governance, Levy, and Deterrent Liability","parent_semantic_invariant":"Rule maintains a household or polity through care, contracts, assessed transfers, testimony, and sanctions that establish responsibility.","parent_surprising_reach":"The Pharaoh precedent activates not only kingship but a full administrative frame of household allegiance, tribute, tax, covenant, wage, surety, and exemplary punishment.","key":"B","name":"Tribute, tax, covenant, and wage","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A polity fixes obligations through covenant and collects assessed value as tribute, tax, loan, or compensated labor.","active_motifs":"tribute or levy paid by a population (`quranic:root_000009:B008/m01`); tax imposed on person or land (`quranic:root_000906:B010/m01`); a loan or deed awaiting return (`quranic:root_001217:B003/m01`); wage and contractual hire (`quranic:root_000015:B001/m01`); covenant and protected agreement (`quranic:root_000532:B011/m01`); mutual promising (`quranic:root_001662:B004/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:18 `وَعْدُهُ`; 73:19-20 `رَبِّهِ/رَبَّكَ/ءَاتُوا/يَضْرِبُونَ/أَقْرِضُوا/قَرْضًا/أَجْرًا`","synthesis":"These transfers differ legally but share institutional assessment. Covenant creates the relation, tribute and tax impose payment, loan anticipates return, and wage compensates service.","ayah_refs":["73:18","73:19"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Animal Drive in Racing and Breeding","parent_semantic_invariant":"Animal force is expressed as directed forward drive, whether toward a finish, a mate, or productive offspring.","parent_surprising_reach":"Messenger-sending, precedence, ease, deterrence, and productive increase are all pressured by a detailed equine and herd mechanics.","key":"B","name":"Stallion signal, estrus, mating, and milk","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A male signals readiness, females enter estrus, mating occurs, and reproduction yields milk.","active_motifs":"stallion’s threatening roar before mounting (`quranic:root_001662:B005/m01`); female animal seeking a male (`quranic:root_000248:B009/m01`); mating of a male camel with a female (`quranic:root_000906:B011/m01`); estrus of a she-camel (`quranic:root_000009:B012/m01`); mating drive in sheep (`quranic:root_001619:B006/m01`); milk and offspring increasing (`quranic:root_001694:B006/m01`); milk flowing continuously (`quranic:root_000563:B006/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:15-16 `أَرْسَلْنَا/ٱلرَّسُولَ/وَبِيلًا`; 73:17 `يَجْعَلُ`; 73:18 `وَعْدُهُ`; 73:20 `ءَاتُوا/يَضْرِبُونَ/تَيَسَّرَ`","synthesis":"Signal, estrus, mating, conception potential, and lactation form a reproductive sequence. Forward drive is redirected from race progress into generative productivity.","ayah_refs":["73:15","73:17","73:18","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Cutting, Shaping, and Material Finish","parent_semantic_invariant":"Raw material becomes a finished object through cutting, striking, grinding, weaving, covering, and surface treatment.","parent_surprising_reach":"The language of action and fulfilled promise activates an artisan workshop containing shears, adze, mallet, grinding stone, textile, pile, coating, and tanning dose.","key":"A","name":"The shaping workshop","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Workers cut and strike material, hew wood, grind solids, and weave a stable form.","active_motifs":"cutting with shears or teeth (`quranic:root_001217:B001/m01`); adze for shaping wood (`quranic:root_001207:B008/m01`); grinding stone (`quranic:root_000879:B008/m01`); workers including carpenter and digger (`quranic:root_001167:B003/m01`); heavy mallet or beating beam (`quranic:root_001619:B004/m01`); well-made weaving (`quranic:root_000217:B007/m01`); tools, striking place, and tent-making (`quranic:root_000906:B014/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:14 `ٱلْجِبَالُ`; 73:16 `وَبِيلًا`; 73:18 `مَفْعُولًا`; 73:20 `يَضْرِبُونَ/صَّلَوٰةَ/قَرْضًا/تُقَدِّمُوا`","synthesis":"Cutting establishes edges, adze and mallet shape mass, grinding reduces particles, and weaving assembles strands. Worker and tool roles make this a single production floor rather than a tool list.","ayah_refs":["73:14","73:16","73:18","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Making, Existing, and Transforming","parent_semantic_invariant":"A thing moves from possibility through creation and occurrence into a changed, identifiable state.","parent_surprising_reach":"Creation, existence, fabrication, and transformation are kept distinct, allowing the fulfilled promise and altered children or mountains to be read as different ontological operations.","key":"A","name":"Creating and bringing into existence","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"An agent originates a thing, which then occurs and stands as an existing object that can be known or found.","active_motifs":"making or creating (`quranic:root_000248:B001/m01`); originating and beginning creation (`quranic:root_001165:B002/m01`); occurrence in time (`quranic:root_001332:B001/m01`); location and standing in existence (`quranic:root_001332:B002/m01`); existence after absence (`quranic:root_001626:B002/m01`); a knowable and reportable thing (`quranic:root_000831:B001/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:14,18,20 `كَانَتِ/كَانَ/يَكُونُ`; 73:17 `يَجْعَلُ`; 73:18 `مُنفَطِرٌ`; 73:19 `شَآءَ`; 73:20 `تَجِدُوهُ`","synthesis":"Creation names agency, occurrence supplies time, place supplies position, and existence makes the result available to knowledge and report. The stages prevent “making” from becoming an undifferentiated synonym for being.","ayah_refs":["73:14","73:17","73:18","73:19","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Making, Existing, and Transforming","parent_semantic_invariant":"A thing moves from possibility through creation and occurrence into a changed, identifiable state.","parent_surprising_reach":"Creation, existence, fabrication, and transformation are kept distinct, allowing the fulfilled promise and altered children or mountains to be read as different ontological operations.","key":"B","name":"Beginning, fabricating, and changing state","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"An agent starts an operation, may construct a false artifact, or transforms an existing object into a new condition.","active_motifs":"beginning and persisting in an act (`quranic:root_000248:B004/m01`); enacted work (`quranic:root_001167:B001/m01`); fabricated composition (`quranic:root_001167:B004/m01`); transforming a thing’s condition (`quranic:root_000248:B002/m01`); derived novelty produced from another thing (`quranic:root_001683:B005/m01`); acting hurriedly (`quranic:root_001434:B004/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:17 `يَجْعَلُ/ٱلْوِلْدَٰنَ/شِيبًا`; 73:18 `مَفْعُولًا`; 73:20 `يَكُونُ`; surface anchor unavailable for `quranic:root_001434:B004` because `م ع ع` is missing from root coverage","synthesis":"Beginning establishes process, work realizes it, and transformation changes an existing patient; fabrication is a rival operation that produces an artifact without truthful correspondence. ## Your response

Write in tr. Produce your response as three parts, in this order. If you return one combined response, label the parts clearly. If an orchestrator asks you to write the parts into separate files, omit wrapper labels from the prose and evidence files; the file path already supplies the label.

1. **The prose** — per the output contract in `COMMENTARY_SPEC.md` §5 and the task document above: continuous, single voice, no provenance markers, no headers named after evidence layers.
2. **The evidence surface** — separate from the prose, addressable per phrase, per `PRINCIPLES.md` §12.
3. A section headed exactly `=== PROMPT FRICTION ===` reporting honestly where the specification above was unclear, self-contradictory, underspecified, or impossible to follow, and any point where you had to invent a rule to proceed. Report this section every time, even if the run went cleanly — say so explicitly if you found nothing. This is how the specification gets improved.

## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision, not additional bulk.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response or
  confidence movement;
- if `v12_focus_trace_hermetic` is present, use its `baseline_models`,
  `context_deltas`, and `surprising_valid_outliers` as reconstructed
  before/after evidence. Do not call it a staged reveal transcript. Preserve
  surprising outliers when they remain anchored in this ayah, especially
  secondary split-root activations;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- treat `root_lexicon` as full dictionary/gloss support, not branch-filtered.
  It uses QAC-to-Furuq root mapping; when a QAC root is split, non-dominant
  Furuq targets are included as additional root entries and recorded in
  coverage. If multiple QAC roots map to the same Furuq root, the shared entry
  records all source roots in `qac_roots_ar` / `qac_root_mappings`; use those
  fields for evidence attribution when present, not the legacy single `root_ar`
  alone. Dictionary/gloss branches are evidence support, not independent
  obligations, unless tied to this ayah's word, topic, reader payoff, or cited
  relation. Full field means all distinct reader payoffs from activated
  material, not every dictionary branch for the root.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. State B channel restrictions still apply.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by activated or cited root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, compressed to its final reader-visible result.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

A paragraph may begin with the ayah's surface, a concrete image, or a
reader-facing claim. It may not begin with a bare grammar label or stacked
abstractions. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi Allah'a verir; sabit ad cümlesi bu hamdi
yerleşik bir hüküm olarak taşır" over "Bu âyet, tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik
bir hüküm kurar."

For this short ayah, keep at least two-thirds of the prose on its own wording.
Compress all later developments into at most three paragraphs and end with one
plain synthesis paragraph.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
include a profile-specific style audit in friction confirming that there was no
live misconception, replacement risk, or counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
